# Consolidation: changes and image decisions

A row is a reading decision at one location; a row may affect both witnesses. Pure macro syntax is excluded. Doubt resolutions are identified in their evidence; unresolved readings remain marked in the transcription.

- 156-4/074, First live diagram: alignment, dots and bottom overwrite [J156-4-10_156-4_scan074_R17]: The first live comparison scheme aligns G beneath H-prime and F beneath G-prime; vertical order signs, dotted continuation and the overwritten bottom capital are retained.

C6 is preferred, while 6b remains possible. The crop does not support 6a or C5.

In (1.7), the literal order is membership X∈tilde F before the bar and Omb X after it, with a comma following the close. The supplied S reading is rejected only if used to replace the diplomatic transcription. Root reports that S’s original discussion itself calls C’s Multomb faithful and presents Omb as an intended-slip interpretation; that distinction is preserved.
